I'll go over and check it, but the floor is completely dry. It, uh, it started right around the winter freeze. The repairs on the house just... they turned into this huge mess that I couldn't get out from under. I'm not really sure. I can't usually make out the exact words. It's more like a low mumbling, just whispering back and forth to each other. It's just always this muffled talking.\n\nDoctor: Did the problems with the house repairs and the stress that came with it seem to trigger or worsen these experiences of hearing and seeing things?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I'd say so. It's just always this muffled talking.\n\nDoctor: Did the problems with the house repairs and the stress that came with it seem to trigger or worsen these experiences of hearing and seeing things?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I'd say so. Before the house problems dragged on and I fell into this depression... I never heard voices in the walls or saw wet patches that weren't there. I never thought the neighbors were tracking me, either. Those things only showed up after I had already stopped caring about my usual life and started feeling like a failure every day. It all just started piling up after that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period where you didn't hear the voices or see things that aren't there, since they first started?\n\nPatient: No, not since they started. Like I was just saying a minute ago, before the house mess really dragged on and I fell into this depression, I never dealt with any of this. I never heard the voices, or saw those wet patches, or worried about the neighbors tracking me. All of that only began after I had already stopped caring about my life and just... felt like a failure all the time. But once those things showed up, no, I haven't had a break from them. I never heard the voices, or saw those wet patches, or worried about the neighbors tracking me. All of that only began after I had already stopped caring about my life and just... felt like a failure all the time. But once those things showed up, no, I haven't had a break from them. I've never had a period where I felt really happy or excited for a long time. It's really just been the exact opposite for me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt extremely energetic or active for a period of time?\n\nPatient: No, not at all. I don't see how they connect to me feeling so down or like a failure. I really don't think so. I don't see a connection. Like I was trying to say, the whispering is just... whispering. I can't make out the words. And the walls are just wet. It doesn't feel like it has anything to do with my mood or... or how bad I feel about myself. It's just happening in the house.\n\nDoctor: Do the voices or visions happen only when you're feeling extremely depressed?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I suppose they do. Before the house problems dragged on and I fell into this depression, I never had anything like this happen. I never heard voices in the walls, or saw wet patches that weren’t really there... or thought the neighbors were tracking me. None of that. \n\nThose things only showed up after I had already stopped caring about my usual life. It all just started happening after I was already feeling like a total failure every single day. I feel like we keep going in circles with this a bit. Like I said a minute ago, I can't even make out the words. So, no, they aren't saying anything about me being a failure. It's just quiet whispering. \n\nAnd the wet patches... they don't show any kind of picture or message or anything like that. They just look like plain old water spots on the wall.
